062_2026-05-21_055739_blofin_1779332116390.png
#63 2026-05-21 06:14:08 discord_message_screenshot Discord open in Discord ↗
He wrote
Someone asked this in my DMs why I don't like taking partials so putting it here is my point of view, of course you don't have to agree just my style. @Paladin Notify
Screenshot of a Discord DM exchange: a member asks why Paladin does not take partial profits on BTC, and Paladin explains his trade-management philosophy: he exits 100% at TP1 because partials get round-tripped while a stop loss is always a 100% loss, his trades are mostly 0.5-1R, and taking full TP makes his R accounting simple (one loss recovered by two 100% TP wins).
Text visible in the image
(first message, author name cropped off at top):
Hey Paladin, just saw you mention you don't like to take partial profits in BTC.

Was just curious why?

Thanks for posting in ScalpX it seems like every trade you take is a home run! Keep it up 💪🏻🤠

Paladin [Will not DM] May 21, 2026 8:06 AM
First of all, appreciate the compliment!
I don't like partials because partials can hurt

For example I take 50% TP then it comes back and hits my BE I wasted 50% of the profits? Okay understandable

But whenever I hit SL I get a 100% SL
You round trip more on partials than you make

Plus I don't do high RR trading, my trades are mostly 0.5-1R trades

I take 100% it helps me keep track of my R, if I lose 1 trade I know I'll make it back by 2 trades 100% TP

Not 4 trades with 3 trades taking TP1 25% and 4th trade finally hitting my TP4
If TP1 is the most confident TP why not exit 100% there instead of round tripping?

Yes you miss big moves to flex on pnl card but I don't have to stress about round trips

Basically SL is 100% in partial TP trading and TPs are 25-50%
Sorry for writing so much I hope you understood
Note: Top of the screenshot is cropped, so the author/avatar and timestamp of the first (question) message are not visible.

Discord screenshot where Paladin explains his R concept: 1R = profit equal to the stop-loss distance, ROI % is irrelevant to it. He gives the BEAT short (went 2R, TP taken at 1.7R) and the live SUI long (from 1, SL 0.95, TP 1.05) as examples, revealing that his SUI trade was a 1:1 risk/reward setup; the accompanying message adds that he risks 1-2% on alts and 3-5% on BTC.
Text visible in the image
Diafonso 6:23 PM
@Paladin [Will not DM] i am confused u said 1R soon, but you are up 150% arent u already 1R?
or in this case 1.5R

Paladin [Will not DM] [SX] [VIP] [OP] 6:29 PM
Noooo
That's ROI
1R is when you make profit equal to your SL.

If SL is 100$ and TP is 100$ that makes 1R, if SL is 100$ and TP is 200$ that makes 2R
If SL is 100$ and TP is 50$ that makes 0.5R

For example we did beat short it went 2R, so SL was 100$ and TP 200$
(We took TP @ 1.7 R)

Basically your trade have to move up same amount up against SL to score 1R

For example you longed SOL at 100
SL is 95, TP is 105
See? SL is -5$, TP is +5$

Just like this rn SUI is running from 1
SL 0.95 (-0.5) TP 1.05 (+0.5)
[reaction: ❤️ 1]
